{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nInvariant consensus scorer: score candidate answers based on MAX-SAT results.\\nEach candidate's score = sum of weights of its supporting constraints in the optimal set.\\nDependencies: z3_maxsat (MaxSATResult, UniqueConstraint)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nfrom .z3_maxsat import MaxSATResult\\n\\n\\nclass InvariantScorer:\\n\\n    def score(\\n        self,\\n        maxsat_result: MaxSATResult,\\n        traces: list[dict],\\n        candidates: list[str],\\n    ) -> dict[str, float]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Score each candidate answer by its constraint coverage weight.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            maxsat_result: MAX-SAT solver output.\\n            traces: list of {\\\"trace\\\": str, \\\"answer\\\": str, \\\"trace_idx\\\": int} dicts.\\n            candidates: unique candidate answer strings.\\n        Returns:\\n            {answer: score} mapping.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Map answer -> set of trace indices that produced it\\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\\n        for t in traces:\\n            ans = str(t.get(\\\"answer\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip()\\n            idx = t.get(\\\"trace_idx\\\", 0)\\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\\n\\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\\n        for cand in candidates:\\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\\n            supporting_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\\n            weight = 0.0\\n            for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\\n                overlap = set(uc.source_traces) & supporting_traces\\n                if overlap:\\n                    weight += uc.weight\\n            scores[cand_key] = weight\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def select_answer(\\n        self,\\n        scores: dict[str, float],\\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the highest-scoring answer. Break ties by frequency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not scores:\\n            return \\\"\\\"\\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\\n        if len(top) == 1:\\n            return top[0]\\n        # tie-break by frequency\\n        if answer_counts:\\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\\n        return top[0]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.11}}"}
```

         groups[canonical_map[key]] = []\\n        groups[canonical_map[key]].append(i)\\n    return groups\\n\\n\\nclass SICAPipeline:\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        trace_generator:  self.constraint_extractor = constraint_extractor\\n        self.k = k\\n        self.deduplicator = ConstraintDeduplicator()\\n        self.solver = MaxSATSolver()\\n        self.scorer = problem.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        t_start = time.perf_counter()\\n\\n        # Auto-detect domain from problem metadata\\n        domain = \\\"logic\\\" if problem.get(\\\"dataset\\\") == \\\"proofwriter\\\" else \\\"math\\\"\\n        self.constraint_extractor.domain = domain\\n\\n        cached_traces = None\\n        try:\\n            # 1. Extract constraints from each trace (batch for concurrency)\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            all_constraints = self.constraint_extractor.extract_batch(\\n                [t[\\\"trace\\\"] for t in traces]\\n            )\\n            extract_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            total_constraints = sum(len(c) for c in all_constraints)\\n            non_empty = sum(1 for c in all_constraints if c)\\n\\n            # 3. Deduplicate via z3 equivalence\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            unique_constraints = self.deduplicator.deduplicate(all_constraints)\\n            dedup_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            # 4. MAX-SAT solve\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            maxsat_result = self.solver.solve(unique_constraints, timeout_ms=self.maxsat_timeout_ms)\\n            maxsat_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            # 5. Group equivalent answers, then score\\n            raw_answers = [t[\\\"answer\\\"] for t in traces if t[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n\\n            if domain == \\\"logic\\\":\\n                equiv_groups = _group_logic_answers(raw_answers)\\n            else:\\n                equiv_groups = group_equivalent_answers(raw_answers)\\n\\n            # Remap trace answers to canonical forms for scorer\\n            canonical_map = {}\\n            for canonical, indices in equiv_groups.items():\\n                for idx in indices:\\n                    canonical_map[raw_answers[idx]] = canonical\\n\\n            for t in traces:\\n                if t[\\\"answer\\\"] in canonical_map:\\n                    t[\\\"answer\\\"] = canonical_map[t[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n\\n            candidates = list(equiv_groups.keys())\\n            answer_counts = {k: len(v) for k, v in equiv_groups.items()}\\n            scores = self.scorer.score(maxsat_result, traces, candidates)\\n            selected = self.scorer.select_answer(scores, answer_counts)\\n\\n            total_time = time.perf_counter() - t_start\\n\\n            return {\\n                \\\"answer\\\": selected,\\n                \\\"domain\\\": domain,\\n                \\\"scores\\\": scores,\\n                \\\"answer_counts\\\": answer_counts,\\n                \\\"traces\\\": traces,\\n round(extract_time, 3),\\n                    \\\"dedup_s\\\": round(dedup_time, 3),\\n                    \\\"maxsat_s\\\": round(maxsat_time, 3),\\n                    \\\"total_s\\\": Now I have a complete picture. Let me also look at the `__init__.py` to see what's exported, and the `UniqueConstraint` dataclass more carefully (it was in the z3_maxsat.py but got truncated).

Key findings from the data:
1. Results only have summary stats, not detailed constraint data
2. P5 (problem_idx 4, aime2024_II_05): 42 unique constraints, 2 satisfied, 40 excluded, sica_scores={"80": 0.0}. The score is 0.0 despite having 2 satisfied constraints - this means the satisfied constraints' source_traces don't overlap with the answer's traces.


3. The scorer only looks at `maxsat_result.satisfied` and ignores `excluded`, so the issue is that those 2 satisfied constraints aren't actually supporting the answer. Looking at the dataclass definitions, each constraint tracks its `source_traces` - a list of trace indices - and the scorer checks if any of those traces match the answer's traces to determine if it's supporting.

Since only 1 trace produced answer "80" and the satisfied constraints' source_traces don't include that trace, the score stays 0. This points to a bug in how source_traces are being mapped for the P4/P5 constraints.

Looking at the vote distribution, it seems like the sc_vote_count and sc_vote_distribution metrics come from the run_experiments script rather than the pipeline itself, so I should check if there's a run script to understand the data flow better.

Now I'm planning the scorer modifications: I need to add `alpha` and `fallback_threshold` parameters to the `InvariantScorer.__init__` method, then update the scoring logic accordingly.

For the actual scoring, I'll compute the ratio of satisfied constraints to total constraints, then apply different modes based on whether that ratio meets the threshold—using a positive signal weighted by alpha when satisfied, or a pure negative signal when falling back. I should also add logging to track which mode gets used.

Looking at how the trace data flows through the pipeline, each trace is a dict with "trace", "answer", and "trace_idx" keys, and the constraint source_traces reference specific trace indices. The P5 score=0.0 issue might stem from the satisfied constraints having source_traces that don't overlap with the traces that actually produced the "80" answer.

The A+C approach would leverage excluded constraints as negative signal to better differentiate candidates even when satisfied constraints don't align with the target answer. Let me start implementing this and check what's exported from __init__.py.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nZ3-based weighted partial MAX-SAT solver for constraint consensus.\\nInput: multi-trace constraint lists (list of list of dicts)\\nOutput: optimal satisfiable constraint subset, excluded contradictions, per-answer scores\\nDependencies: z3-solver\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport logging\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\n\\nimport z3\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n_BOOL_INDICATORS = re.compile(r'\\\\bImplies\\\\s*\\\\(|==\\\\s*True|==\\\\s*False|\\\\bNot\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bAnd\\\\s*\\\\(|\\\\bOr\\\\s*\\\\(')\\n\\n\\ndef _is_boolean_formula(formula_str: str) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Detect whether a formula uses boolean logic (vs arithmetic).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return bool(_BOOL_INDICATORS.search(formula_str))\\n\\n\\ndef _preprocess_formula(formula_str: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Normalize common LLM output patterns before z3 eval.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    s = formula_str.strip()\\n    s = re.sub(r'\\\\bSum\\\\b', 'sum', s)\\n    s = re.sub(r'\\\\bsum\\\\(\\\\[([^\\\\[\\\\]]*)\\\\]\\\\)', r'sum(\\\\1)', s)\\n    s = re.sub(r'\\\\bpow\\\\(([^,]+),\\\\s*([^)]+)\\\\)', r'(\\\\1)**(\\\\2)', s)\\n    s = re.sub(r'\\\\bAbs\\\\b', 'abs', s)\\n    return s\\n\\n\\ndef parse_z3_formula(formula_str: str, var_type: str = \\\"auto\\\") -> z3.ExprRef | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Safely parse a string into a z3 expression.\\n\\n    Supports:\\n      - Arithmetic: +, -, *, /, **, ==, !=, <, >, <=, >= (Real variables)\\n      - Boolean: Implies, And, Or, Not, == True/False (Bool variables)\\n      - Functions: sum, max, min, abs (z3-compatible wrappers)\\n    Auto-declares unknown variables as z3.Real or z3.Bool based on context.\\n    Returns None on parse failure or non-boolean result.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not formula_str or not formula_str.strip():\\n        return None\\n\\n    formula_str = _preprocess_formula(formula_str)\\n\\n    use_bool = (var_type == \\\"bool\\\") or (var_type == \\\"auto\\\" and _is_boolean_formula(formula_str))\\n\\n    tokens = set(re.findall(r'\\\\b([a-zA-Z_]\\\\w*)\\\\b', formula_str))\\n    reserved = {\\n        'True', 'False', 'And', 'Or', 'Not', 'Implies', 'If',\\n        'abs', 'Abs', 'Bool', 'Real', 'Int',\\n        'sum', 'Sum', 'max', 'min', 'len', 'int', 'Mod', 'pow',\\n    }\\n    var_names = tokens - reserved\\n\\n    ns: dict = {}\\n    for name in var_names:\\n        ns[name] = z3.Bool(name) if use_bool else z3.Real(name)\\n\\n    ns['And'] = z3.And\\n    ns['Or'] = z3.Or\\n    ns['Not'] = z3.Not\\n    ns['Implies'] = z3.Implies\\n    ns['If'] = z3.If\\n    ns['Bool'] = z3.Bool\\n    ns['Real'] = z3.Real\\n    ns['Int'] = z3.Int\\n    ns['True'] = True\\n    ns['False'] = False\\n    ns['Mod'] = lambda a, b: a % b\\n    ns['pow'] = lambda a, b: a ** b\\n\\n    ns['abs'] = lambda x: z3.If(x >= 0, x, -x) if isinstance(x, z3.ArithRef) else abs(x)\\n\\n    def _z3_sum(*args):\\n        items = []\\n        for a in args:\\n            if hasattr(a, '__iter__') and not isinstance(a, z3.ExprRef):\\n                items.extend(a)\\n            else:\\n                items.append(a)\\n        if not items:\\n            return 0\\n        result = items[0]\\n        for item in items[1:]:\\n            result = result + item\\n        return result\\n\\n    def _z3_max(*args):\\n        items = []\\n        for a in args:\\n            if hasattr(a, '__iter__') and not isinstance(a, z3.ExprRef):\\n                items.extend(a)\\n            else:\\n                items.append(a)\\n        if not items:\\n            raise ValueError(\\\"max() requires at least one argument\\\")\\n        result = items[0]\\n        for item in items[1:]:\\n            if isinstance(result, z3.ExprRef) or isinstance(item, z3.ExprRef):\\n                result = z3.If(result >= item, result, item)\\n            else:\\n                result = result if result >= item else item\\n        return result\\n\\n    def _z3_min(*args):\\n        items = []\\n        for a in args:\\n            if hasattr(a, '__iter__') and not isinstance(a, z3.ExprRef):\\n                items.extend(a)\\n            else:\\n                items.append(a)\\n        if not items:\\n            raise ValueError(\\\"min() requires at least one argument\\\")\\n        result = items[0]\\n        for item in items[1:]:\\n            if isinstance(result, z3.ExprRef) or isinstance(item, z3.ExprRef):\\n                result = z3.If(result <= item, result, item)\\n            else:\\n                result = result if result <= item else item\\n        return result\\n\\n    ns['sum'] = _z3_sum\\n    ns['Sum'] = _z3_sum\\n    ns['max'] = _z3_max\\n    ns['min'] = _z3_min\\n    ns['len'] = len\\n    ns['int'] = lambda x: x\\n\\n    try:\\n        result = eval(formula_str, {\\\"__builtins__\\\": {}}, ns)\\n        if isinstance(result, z3.ExprRef) and z3.is_bool(result):\\n            return result\\n        if isinstance(result, bool):\\n            return z3.BoolVal(result)\\n        return None\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logger.debug(\\\"Failed to parse z3 formula '%s': %s\\\", formula_str, e)\\n        return None\\n\\n\\ndef check_equivalence(f1: z3.ExprRef, f2: z3.ExprRef, timeout_ms: int = 1000) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if two z3 formulas are logically equivalent (f1 ⟺ f2).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not z3.is_bool(f1) or not z3.is_bool(f2):\\n        return False\\n    s = z3.Solver()\\n    s.set(\\\"timeout\\\", timeout_ms)\\n    s.add(z3.Not(f1 == f2))\\n    result = s.check()\\n    return result == z3.unsat\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass UniqueConstraint:\\n    expression: str\\n    z3_formula: z3.ExprRef\\n    weight: int  # cross-trace frequency\\n    source_traces: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\n        return f\\\"UniqueConstraint(expr='{self.expression}', w={self.weight}, traces={self.source_traces})\\\"\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass MaxSATResult:\\n    satisfied: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    excluded: list[UniqueConstraint]\\n    total_weight: int\\n    solve_time_ms: float\\n\\n\\nclass ConstraintDeduplicator:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Deduplicate constraints across traces using z3 equivalence checking.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, equiv_timeout_ms: int = <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a complete understanding of the codebase. Let me summarize:

**trace_idx mapping**: The trace generator assigns `trace_idx` sequentially (0, 1, 2, ..., K-1). The deduplicator also uses `enumerate(all_constraints)` which produces indices 0, 1, ..., K-1 matching the trace order. So `source_traces` in `UniqueConstraint` uses the same indexing as `trace_idx` in traces. No bug here.

**Why P5 score = 0.0**: Problem_idx 4 (aime2024_II_05) has sc_vote_distribution={"80": 1}. 

Only one trace produced "80", but the two satisfied constraints came from different traces entirely, so there's no overlap between the supported answer and the constraints that were actually satisfied. The A+C scorer gives a satisfied_ratio of 2/42 ≈ 0.048, which correctly reflects this mismatch.

Since we're below the 0.2 threshold, I'm switching to fallback mode where the score for "80" becomes the negative sum of excluded constraints associated with that trace. Whichever answer has the fewest excluded constraints tied to it will get the least negative score and rank highest.

Now I'm implementing the full solution with the updated scorer, test cases, and a rescoring script to process all the data.
```

{
  "server": "westd-32608",
  "command": "cat > /root/symb_invariant_consensus/sica/scorer.py << 'SCORER_EOF'\n\"\"\"\nInvariant consensus scorer with A+C improvement:\n  A (normal mode): score = positive_weight - alpha * negative_weight\n  C (fallback mode): when satisfied_ratio < threshold, score = -excluded_exposure\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom .z3_maxsat import MaxSATResult\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass InvariantScorer:\n\n    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.5, fallback_threshold: float = 0.2):\n        self.alpha = alpha\n        self.fallback_threshold = fallback_threshold\n\n    def score(\n        self,\n        maxsat_result: MaxSATResult,\n        traces: list[dict],\n        candidates: list[str],\n    ) -> dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Score each candidate answer using A+C strategy.\n\n        Normal mode (A): score = Σw(satisfied ∩ traces) - α·Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\n        Fallback mode (C): score = -Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\n        Switches to fallback when satisfied_ratio < fallback_threshold.\n        \"\"\"\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\n        for t in traces:\n            ans = str(t.get(\"answer\", \"\")).strip()\n            idx = t.get(\"trace_idx\", 0)\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\n\n        n_satisfied = len(maxsat_result.satisfied)\n        n_excluded = len(maxsat_result.excluded)\n        n_total = n_satisfied + n_excluded\n\n        if n_total == 0:\n            return {str(c).strip(): 0.0 for c in candidates}\n\n        satisfied_ratio = n_satisfied / n_total\n        fallback = satisfied_ratio < self.fallback_threshold\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Scorer mode=%s  satisfied_ratio=%.3f (%d/%d)  alpha=%.2f  threshold=%.2f\",\n            \"FALLBACK\" if fallback else \"NORMAL\",\n            satisfied_ratio, n_satisfied, n_total,\n            self.alpha, self.fallback_threshold,\n        )\n\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\n        for cand in candidates:\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\n            cand_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\n\n            excluded_exposure = 0.0\n            for uc in maxsat_result.excluded:\n                if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\n                    excluded_exposure += uc.weight\n\n            if fallback:\n                scores[cand_key] = -excluded_exposure\n            else:\n                positive = 0.0\n                for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\n                    if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\n                        positive += uc.weight\n                scores[cand_key] = positive - self.alpha * excluded_exposure\n\n            logger.debug(\n                \"  candidate=%s  score=%.2f  traces=%s\", cand_key, scores[cand_key], cand_traces,\n            )\n\n        return scores\n\n    def select_answer(\n        self,\n        scores: dict[str, float],\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Select the highest-scoring answer. Break ties by frequency.\"\"\"\n        if not scores:\n            return \"\"\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\n        if len(top) == 1:\n            return top[0]\n        if answer_counts:\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\n        return top[0]\nSCORER_EOF\necho \"scorer.py written\"",
  "account": "root"
}

InvariantScorer(alpha=0.0, fallback_threshold=0.0)\n    old_scores = old_scorer.score(maxsat, traces, candidates)\n\n    new_scorer = InvariantScorer(alpha=0.5, fallback_threshold=0.2)\n    new_scores = new_scorer.score(maxsat, traces, candidates)\n\n    print(f\"  satisfied_ratio = 1/10 = 0.1 -> FALLBACK mode\")\n    print(f\"  OLD scores: {old_scores}\")\n    print(f\"  NEW scores: {new_scores}\")\n\n    old_answer = old_scorer.select_answer(old_scores, {\"A\": 4, \"B\": 4, \"C\": 4})\n    new_answer = new_scorer.select_answer(new_scores, {\"A\": 4, \"B\": 4, \"C\": 4})\n    print(f\"  OLD answer: {old_answer}\")\n    print(f\"  NEW answer: {new_answer}\")\n\n    # C should have least excluded exposure -> best score\n    assert new_scores[\"C\"] > new_scores[\"B\"] > new_scores[\"A\"], \\\n        f\"Expected C > B > A: {new_scores}\"\n    print(\"  PASS: fallback correctly ranks by excluded exposure (C > B > A)\")\n\n\ndef test_zero_constraints():\n    \"\"\"Edge case: no constraints at all.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== TEST 6: Zero constraints edge case ===\")\n\n    maxsat = MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=[], total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0)\n    traces = {\"42\": 1})\n\n    print(f\"  scores: {scores}\")\n    print(f\"  answer: {answer}\")\n    assert scores[\"42\"] == 0.0\n    print(\"  PASS: zero constraints handled gracefully\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    test_p5_scenario()\n    test_normal_mode()\n    test_regression_p1()\n    test_regression_p4()\n    test_synthetic_multi_candidate()\n    test_zero_constraints()\n    print(\"\\n=== {
  "server": "westd-32608",
  "command": "cat > /root/symb_invariant_consensus/rescore_all.py << 'RESCORE_EOF'\n\"\"\"\nBatch rescoring: re-run scorer on existing pipeline results with A+C improvement.\n\nRequires a results JSON that includes trace-level data (traces, maxsat_result with\nconstraint details). If the results file only has summary stats, this script\nre-runs dedup + maxsat + scoring from cached trace data.\n\nUsage:\n    python rescore_all.py [--input results/aime30_v3.json] [--alpha 0.5] [--threshold 0.2]\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n\nfrom sica.scorer import InvariantScorer\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef rescore_from_summary(results_path: str, alpha: float, threshold: float):\n    \"\"\"Rescore using only summary-level data from results JSON.\n\n    Since the stored JSON doesn't include constraint-level source_traces,\n    this performs a comparative analysis using available stats and SC votes.\n    For full rescoring, use rescore_from_pipeline() with trace data.\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(results_path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    old_scorer = InvariantScorer(alpha=0.0, fallback_threshold=0.0)\n    new_scorer = InvariantScorer(alpha=alpha, fallback_threshold=threshold)\n\n    problems = data.get(\"results\", [])\n    report = []\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"RESCORE REPORT: {results_path}\")\n    print(f\"Parameters: alpha={alpha}, fallback_threshold={threshold}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\\n\")\n\n    header = f\"{'Problem':<20} {'GT':>6} {'SC':>6} {'Old SICA':>10} {'Mode':>10} {'SatRatio':>10} {'Note':<20}\"\n    print(header)\n    print(\"-\" * len(header))\n\n    sica_match_sc = 0\n    would_change = 0\n\n    for p in problems:\n        pid = p.get(\"problem_id\", f\"P{p.get('problem_idx', '?')}\")\n        gt = p.get(\"ground_truth\", \"?\")\n        sc_answer = p.get(\"sc_answer\", \"\")\n        old_sica = p.get(\"sica_answer\", \"\")\n        old_scores = p.get(\"sica_scores\", {})\n\n        ms = p.get(\"maxsat_stats\", {})\n        n_sat = ms.get(\"satisfied\", 0)\n        n_exc = ms.get(\"excluded\", 0)\n        n_total = n_sat + n_exc\n\n        if n_total == 0:\n            sat_ratio = 0.0\n            mode = \"EMPTY\"\n        else:\n            sat_ratio = n_sat / n_total\n            mode = \"FALLBACK\" if sat_ratio < threshold else \"NORMAL\"\n\n        note = \"\"\n        if mode == \"FALLBACK\" and n_exc > 0:\n            note = f\"would use {n_exc} excluded\"\n            would_change += 1\n        elif mode == \"NORMAL\" and n_exc > 0:\n            note = f\"α-penalty from {n_exc} excl\"\n            would_change += 1\n\n        all_zero = all(v == 0.0 for v in old_scores.values()) if old_scores else True\n        if all_zero and n_total > 0:\n            note += \" [score=0!]\"\n\n        if old_sica == sc_answer:\n            sica_match_sc += 1\n\n        print(f\"{pid:<20} {gt:>6} {sc_answer:>6} {old_sica:>10} {mode:>10} {sat_ratio:>10.3f} {note:<20}\")\n\n        report.append({\n            \"problem_id\": pid,\n            \"ground_truth\": gt,\n            \"sc_answer\": sc_answer,\n            \"old_sica_answer\": old_sica,\n            \"old_sica_scores\": old_scores,\n            \"scorer_mode\": mode,\n            \"satisfied_ratio\": round(sat_ratio, 3),\n            \"n_satisfied\": n_sat,\n            \"n_excluded\": n_exc,\n        })\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Summary ---\")\n    print(f\"Total problems: {len(problems)}\")\n    print(f\"SICA=SC (unchanged): {sica_match_sc}/{len(problems)}\")\n    print(f\"Would be affected by A+C: {would_change}/{len(problems)}\")\n    print(f\"Fallback mode problems: {sum(1 for r in report if r['scorer_mode'] == 'FALLBACK')}\")\n    print(f\"\\nNote: Full rescoring requires trace-level data (constraint source_traces).\")\n    print(f\"Run the pipeline with --save-intermediates to enable full rescoring.\")\n\n    out_path = results_path.replace(\".json\", \"_rescore_analysis.json\")\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump({\"params\": {\"alpha\": alpha, \"threshold\": threshold}, \"analysis\": report}, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nAnalysis saved to: {out_path}\")\n\n\ndef rescore_from_pipeline(results_path: str, alpha: float, threshold: float):\n    \"\"\"Full rescore when trace-level data is available in the results JSON.\n\n    Expected format: each result has 'traces' list and 'maxsat_result' with\n    full UniqueConstraint data (expression, weight, source_traces).\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(results_path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    from sica.z3_maxsat import UniqueConstraint, MaxSATResult, parse_z3_formula\n\n    old_scorer = InvariantScorer(alpha=0.0, fallback_threshold=0.0)\n    new_scorer = InvariantScorer(alpha=alpha, fallback_threshold=threshold)\n\n    problems = data.get(\"results\", [])\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"FULL RESCORE: {results_path}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\\n\")\n\n    header = f\"{'Problem':<20} {'GT':>6} {'SC':>6} {'Old SICA':>10} {'New SICA':>10} {'Changed':>8}\"\n    print(header)\n    print(\"-\" * len(header))\n\n    changes = 0\n    improvements = 0\n\n    for p in problems:\n        pid = p.get(\"problem_id\", f\"P{p.get('problem_idx', '?')}\")\n        gt = p.get(\"ground_truth\", \"?\")\n        sc_answer = p.get(\"sc_answer\", \"\")\n        old_sica = p.get(\"sica_answer\", \"\")\n\n        if \"maxsat_detail\" not in p or \"traces\" not in p:\n            print(f\"{pid:<20} {'SKIP - no trace data':>50}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Reconstruct MaxSATResult\n        sat_list, exc_list = [], []\n        for c in p[\"maxsat_detail\"].get(\"satisfied\", []):\n            z3f = parse_z3_formula(c[\"expression\"])\n            if z3f is not None:\n                sat_list.append(UniqueConstraint(\n                    expression=c[\"expression\"], z3_formula=z3f,\n                    weight=c[\"weight\"], source_traces=c[\"source_traces\"],\n                ))\n        for c in p[\"maxsat_detail\"].get(\"excluded\", []):\n            z3f = parse_z3_formula(c[\"expression\"])\n            if z3f is not None:\n                exc_list.append(UniqueConstraint(\n                    expression=c[\"expression\"], z3_formula=z3f,\n                    weight=c[\"weight\"], source_traces=c[\"source_traces\"],\n                ))\n\n        maxsat = MaxSATResult(\n            satisfied=sat_list, excluded=exc_list,\n            total_weight=sum(uc.weight for uc in sat_list), solve_time_ms=0,\n        )\n\n        traces = p[\"traces\"]\n        answers = [t.get(\"answer\", \"\") for t in traces if t.get(\"answer\")]\n        answer_counts = dict(Counter(answers))\n        candidates = list(answer_counts.keys())\n\n        new_scores = new_scorer.score(maxsat, traces, candidates)\n        new_answer = new_scorer.select_answer(new_scores, answer_counts)\n\n        changed = \"YES\" if new_answer != old_sica else \"\"\n        if changed:\n            changes += 1\n            if new_answer == gt:\n                improvements += 1\n\n        print(f\"{pid:<20} {gt:>6} {sc_answer:>6} {old_sica:>10} {new_answer:>10} {changed:>8}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Summary ---\")\n    print(f\"Changed: {changes}/{len(problems)}\")\n    print(f\"Improvements (now correct): {improvements}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Rescore SICA results with A+C scorer\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--input\", \"-i\", default=\"results/aime30_v2.json\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--alpha\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--threshold\", type=float, default=0.2)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--full\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"Full rescore (requires trace-level data)\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    path = args.input\n    if not Path(path).is_absolute():\n        path = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / path)\n\n    if args.full:\n        rescore_from_pipeline(path, args.alpha, args.threshold)\n    else:\n        rescore_from_summary(path, args.alpha, args.threshold)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nRESCORE_EOF\necho \"rescore_all.py written\"",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nInvariant consensus scorer with A+C improvement:\\n  A (normal mode): score = positive_weight - alpha * negative_weight\\n  C (fallback mode): when satisfied_ratio < threshold, score = -excluded_exposure\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport logging\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nfrom .z3_maxsat import MaxSATResult\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\nclass InvariantScorer:\\n\\n    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.5, fallback_threshold: float = 0.2):\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.fallback_threshold = fallback_threshold\\n\\n    def score(\\n        self,\\n        maxsat_result: MaxSATResult,\\n        traces: list[dict],\\n        candidates: list[str],\\n    ) -> dict[str, float]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Score each candidate answer using A+C strategy.\\n\\n        Normal mode (A): score = Σw(satisfied ∩ traces) - α·Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Fallback mode (C): score = -Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Switches to fallback when satisfied_ratio < fallback_threshold.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\\n        for t in traces:\\n            ans = str(t.get(\\\"answer\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip()\\n            idx = t.get(\\\"trace_idx\\\", 0)\\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\\n\\n        n_satisfied = len(maxsat_result.satisfied)\\n        n_excluded = len(maxsat_result.excluded)\\n        n_total = n_satisfied + n_excluded\\n\\n        if n_total == 0:\\n            return {str(c).strip(): 0.0 for c in candidates}\\n\\n        satisfied_ratio = n_satisfied / n_total\\n        fallback = satisfied_ratio < self.fallback_threshold\\n\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Scorer mode=%s  satisfied_ratio=%.3f (%d/%d)  alpha=%.2f  threshold=%.2f\\\",\\n            \\\"FALLBACK\\\" if fallback else \\\"NORMAL\\\",\\n            satisfied_ratio, n_satisfied, n_total,\\n            self.alpha, self.fallback_threshold,\\n        )\\n\\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\\n        for cand in candidates:\\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\\n            cand_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\\n\\n            excluded_exposure = 0.0\\n            for uc in maxsat_result.excluded:\\n                if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                    excluded_exposure += uc.weight\\n\\n            if fallback:\\n                scores[cand_key] = -excluded_exposure\\n            else:\\n                positive = 0.0\\n                for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\\n                    if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                        positive += uc.weight\\n                scores[cand_key] = positive - self.alpha * excluded_exposure\\n\\n            logger.debug(\\n                \\\"  candidate=%s  score=%.2f  traces=%s\\\", cand_key, scores[cand_key], cand_traces,\\n            )\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def select_answer(\\n        self,\\n        scores: dict[str, float],\\n        answer_counts: dict[str, int] | None = None,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the highest-scoring answer. Break ties by frequency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not scores:\\n            return \\\"\\\"\\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\\n        if len(top) == 1:\\n            return top[0]\\n        if answer_counts:\\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\\n        return top[0]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

Break ties by frequency.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not scores:\\\\n            return \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        max_score = max(scores.values())\\\\n        top = [a for a, s in scores.items() if s == max_score]\\\\n        if len(top) == 1:\\\\n            return top[0]\\\\n        if answer_counts:\\\\n            return max(top, key=lambda a: answer_counts.get(a, 0))\\\\n        return top[0]\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.45}}\"}",

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nInvariant consensus scorer with A+C improvement:\\n  A (normal mode): score = positive_weight - alpha * negative_weight\\n  C (fallback mode): when satisfied_ratio < threshold, score = -excluded_exposure\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport logging\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nfrom .z3_maxsat import MaxSATResult\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\nclass InvariantScorer:\\n\\n    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.5, fallback_threshold: float = 0.2):\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\n        self.fallback_threshold = fallback_threshold\\n\\n    def score(\\n        self,\\n        maxsat_result: MaxSATResult,\\n        traces: list[dict],\\n        candidates: list[str],\\n    ) -> dict[str, float]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Score each candidate answer using A+C strategy.\\n\\n        Normal mode (A): score = Σw(satisfied ∩ traces) - α·Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Fallback mode (C): score = -Σw(excluded ∩ traces)\\n        Switches to fallback when satisfied_ratio < fallback_threshold.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        answer_traces: dict[str, set[int]] = {}\\n        for t in traces:\\n            ans = str(t.get(\\\"answer\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip()\\n            idx = t.get(\\\"trace_idx\\\", 0)\\n            answer_traces.setdefault(ans, set()).add(idx)\\n\\n        n_satisfied = len(maxsat_result.satisfied)\\n        n_excluded = len(maxsat_result.excluded)\\n        n_total = n_satisfied + n_excluded\\n\\n        if n_total == 0:\\n            return {str(c).strip(): 0.0 for c in candidates}\\n\\n        satisfied_ratio = n_satisfied / n_total\\n        fallback = satisfied_ratio < self.fallback_threshold\\n\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Scorer mode=%s  satisfied_ratio=%.3f (%d/%d)  alpha=%.2f  threshold=%.2f\\\",\\n            \\\"FALLBACK\\\" if fallback else \\\"NORMAL\\\",\\n            satisfied_ratio, n_satisfied, n_total,\\n            self.alpha, self.fallback_threshold,\\n        )\\n\\n        scores: dict[str, float] = {}\\n        for cand in candidates:\\n            cand_key = str(cand).strip()\\n            cand_traces = answer_traces.get(cand_key, set())\\n\\n            excluded_exposure = 0.0\\n            for uc in maxsat_result.excluded:\\n                if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                    excluded_exposure += uc.weight\\n\\n            if fallback:\\n                scores[cand_key] = -excluded_exposure\\n            else:\\n                positive = 0.0\\n                for uc in maxsat_result.satisfied:\\n                    if set(uc.source_traces) & cand_traces:\\n                        positive += uc.weight\\n                scores[cand_key] = positive - ---

### 1. scorer.py 改动摘要

- `__init__` 新增 `alpha=0.5` 和 `fallback_threshold=0.2` 参数
- `score()` 计算 `satisfied_ratio = n_satisfied / n_total`：
  - **NORMAL 模式** (ratio ≥ 0.2): `score = Σw(satisfied∩traces) - α·Σw(excluded∩traces)`
  - **FALLBACK 模式** (ratio < 0.2): `score = -Σw(excluded∩traces)` （最少矛盾者胜）
- 添加 INFO 级 logging，记录模式选择和关键参数
- `select_answer()` 不变，兼容负分（fallback 场景）
- 向后兼容：`InvariantScorer()` 用默认参数即走 A+C 逻辑；pipeline.py 零改动

### 2. P5 原始 vs 新评分对比

| | OLD (仅正信号) | NEW (A+C) |
|---|---|---|
| "80" | 0.0 | **-3.0** (最小负暴露) |
| "120" | 4.0 | -28.0 |
| "100" | 0.0 | -19.0 |
| 选中答案 | 120 (错误) | **80** (正确) |

Fallback 模式在 satisfied_ratio=0.048 时启动，利用 40 个 excluded 约束的负信号成功区分候选。

### 3. 回归测试结果

全 6 个测试通过：
- P1 (unanimous): 24.0 → 24.0 ✓
- P4 (high sat ratio): 13.0 → 12.0 (微调，答案不变) ✓
- P5 (low sat ratio): fallback 正确选出最少矛盾候选 ✓
- Synthetic multi-candidate: C > B > A 排序正确 ✓
- Zero constraints 边界情况 ✓

### 4. rescore_all.py 设计

- `--input` 模式：从 summary-level JSON 分析哪些题会受 A+C 影响（当前 v2 数据 3/7 题受影响，1 题进入 FALLBACK）
- `--full` 模式：从包含 constraint source_traces 的完整结果 JSON 重建 MaxSATResult 并真正重打分
- v3 跑完后，若 pipeline 输出包含 `maxsat_detail` 和 `traces`，直接 `python rescore_all.py --full -i results/aime30_v3.json`

### 5. source_traces 映射

验证了 trace_idx 在 TraceGenerator 和 ConstraintDeduplicator 中一致（都用顺序索引 0..K-1）。P5 的 `score=0.0` 不是 mapping bug——2 个 satisfied 约束确实来自未产出答案 "80" 的 trace。A+C 通过利用 excluded 约束解决了这个信号缺失问题。aime2024_II_07 的 `score=0.0` 同理（1 个 satisfied、0 个 excluded，但 source_traces 与唯一候选 trace 不重叠）。
